\subsection{53. Where the energy-row floor of section 50 comes from (2026-09-30; the cause is in section 58)}\label{53-where-the-energy-row-floor-of-section-50-comes-from-2026-09-30-the-cause-is-in-section-58}

The floor of section 50 (hydrodynamic solves ending at the iteration cap
with \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 1e-5 to 1e-4 against the energy tolerance 1e-6) was measured on
the 0.06 conduction rung at factor 0.773561 (IC: pass state
r20260930T172935.418\_c1790756975418473\_p22 of
\texttt{atomic\_scalar\_gj1132x0.06\_kzz1e9\_HeH9.7\_ser2\_cond08\_s0.773561}; runs in
\texttt{.P1/atomic97/diag\_R/floor\_*}, binaries \texttt{EXHALE\_9d0b8d4c.x} and, for the
new diagnostics below, \texttt{EXHALE\_dfb51387.x}). Every run starts at the same
\textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} = 3.882e-5, energy row of cell 296.

\textbf{The row.} At cells 290 to 312 (r = 1.75 to 2.06 R\_p) the energy row is
the difference of the advective divergence and the conduction source, each
\textasciitilde1e-10 in the row\textquotesingle s units, with heating \textasciitilde4e-12; the residual is +-1e-15 to
4e-15, alternating in sign from cell to cell, i.e. 1e-5 to 4e-5 of the row\textquotesingle s
scale. The temperature there is 748 K and flat: the relative first
difference of T is 4.3e-5 to 5.3e-5 per cell and the relative second
difference 1.4e-6 to 2.1e-6 per cell. The H ionization crosses x(H+) = 0.5
between cells 306 and 307.

\textbf{Excluded, by measurement.}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
{\small
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
candidate & test & result \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
acceptance rule & \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_SER\_RNORM} (section 50) & same floor \\
scaling of the linear model & \texttt{EXHALE\_JUDGED\_ROWS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_JUDGED\_MERIT} & cap at 1.5e-5 / 5.3e-5 \\
probe length of the Jacobian action & \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_PROBE\_ARC} = 10, 100; \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_COLUMN\_SCALE=1} & 40 iterations, best 8.2e-6 / 1.0e-5 / 5.4e-5, no descent \\
frozen WENO3 weights in the model & new \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_FRESH\_WEIGHTS=1} (products with the weights recomputed) & first 9 iterations identical to 4 digits: the Krylov solve takes 1 product, the step is the banded preconditioner\textquotesingle s \\
tolerance of the per-cell ionization solve & \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL} = 1e-12, 1e-14 & start \\
sonic point, Roe entropy fix & Mach number of the band & 0.005 to 0.013: subsonic, the Roe flux is smooth there \\
\end{longtable}
}
}

The additivity test (\texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_ADDITIVITY=1}) puts its defect in cells 1
to 32 (mass and momentum rows; along directions off the Krylov basis the
second difference does not scale with the arc at cell 1), not in the band.
That non-smoothness at the base is recorded here and not pursued.

\textbf{Located.} \texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS=1} now prints, cell by cell over a band
(\texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS\_CELLS=lo,hi}, default 290,312), the energy row F, the
model\textquotesingle s F + J dY, the row actually found at Y + dY, their ratio, and the
difference quotient (F(Y + t dY) - F)/t for t = 0.1 to 1e5. (The
"linear residual left over the row of F" printed before is the column
maximum in raw units, which the base cells set; it says nothing about this
band.) On the first iteration:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  cells 290 to 304 and 308 to 312: actual change / predicted = 0.90 to
  1.13, the row falls by \textasciitilde10 per step;
\item
  cells 305, 306, 307: 1.91, 2.05, 1.41; the row changes sign at the same
  magnitude (306: +1.42e-15 -\textgreater{} -1.49e-15) and does so again on the next
  iteration (ratios 2.03, 4.62, 0.43).
\end{itemize}

The difference quotient along dY is constant within 10 to 20 per cent for
t \textgreater= 10 and at t = 0.1, and doubles at t = 1 in cells 305 to 307 only
(306: -1.28, \textbf{-2.91}, -1.59, -1.39, -1.43, -1.42, -1.44 e-15): the row
carries a deterministic jump of 0.6 to 1.5e-15 between states 1e-12 apart,
i.e. 1e-5 of its scale. Heating and cooling in cell 306 jump by 3e-11
(relative) at the same t, and the H I / H II fractions of cells 306 and 307
by 1e-11 to 2e-11, ten times their neighbors\textquotesingle, with a relative step of
\textasciitilde1e-12 in every cell.

\textbf{The source.} With \texttt{EXHALE\_EQ\_MEASURE\_TRACE=1} every residual evaluation
stops the composition elimination after 1 pass, on a particle-count
increment of 6e-12 at cell 307 (the closure target is 1e-8). Forcing more
passes (\texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_EQ\_TOL=1e-15}, \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_MAX=12}), the
increment at cell 307 stays at 3.0e-11 for all 12 passes: the elimination
does not converge there. \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_REPORT\_CELL=307} shows x(H+) of that
cell alternating between two values on successive passes,

\begin{verbatim}
5.030758860692102E-01   5.030758860599341E-01   (difference 9.3e-12)
\end{verbatim}

each returned with a reaction residual of 1e-16 and solver info 1; with the
per-cell tolerance at 1e-15 the two values are the same to the last digit
and the solver returns info 4 (no further progress). \emph{Likely} the elimination alternates between two nearby states. It is
not established that the per-cell residual is flat (section 54): the
printed residual is the normalized one, whose hydrogen turnover bound
includes the Penning term with the total He density, and successive
passes change the coefficients of the cell problem (T, n\_e, attenuation),
so the two x values can be roots of two slightly different problems.

\textbf{The amplification.} An indeterminacy of 1e-11 in the composition is an
indeterminacy of 1e-11 in the particle count and in T = p/((n\_tot + n\_e) k).
Where the row is the balance of advection and conduction on a profile whose
relative curvature is 1.7e-6 per cell, that moves the conduction divergence
by 1e-11/1.7e-6 = 6e-6 of itself, which is the row\textquotesingle s scale. MEASURED jumps
0.6 to 1.5e-15 against a scale of 1e-10 agree with this. Newton cannot
bring the row below the jump; the solve therefore wanders at 1e-5 to 1e-4
and a rung certifies only when one pass lands inside the tolerance
(section 50).

\textbf{The same place in the other chains.} The worst energy cell of the last
40 iterations against the x(H+) = 0.5 crossing:

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
{\small
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
rung & worst energy cells (count of 40) & crossing between cells \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
0.0624, factor 0.913 & 297 (30), 290, 296, 292 & 296 / 297 \\
photochemical descent, x = 0.0634 & 287 (20), 284, 282, 299 & 288 / 289 \\
0.06, factor 0.774 & 301 (10), 303, 308, 307 & 306 / 307 \\
\end{longtable}
}
}

\textbf{Not decided, for the user.} (i) Condition the per-cell ionization
residual at x(H+) \textasciitilde{} 0.5, so that the composition is determined to rounding
there. (ii) The energy row\textquotesingle s certification scale in a conduction-dominated
layer: it is the largest of the row\textquotesingle s terms, and the conduction term is
itself the difference of two face fluxes that are, by the first and second
differences above, \textasciitilde30 times larger; measured against the face fluxes the
same jump would be \textasciitilde2e-7 of scale (an estimate from those differences, not
measured). Neither was changed. Remedy (ii) is withdrawn (section 54): a
larger denominator admits a 30 times larger absolute imbalance and does not
make the physical residual smaller.

\textbf{Also seen.} \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_TRACE=8} from a second seed (the column\textquotesingle s
composition shifted by five cells) converges at fixed Y to a different
composition (max relative difference 4.8 at cell 266, \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 1.78 against
3.9e-5). The shifted seed changes the elemental totals too (hydrogen at
cell 144 by 37 per cent, section 54), so the two runs solve different
elemental problems. They do not show multiple fixed points of one
problem.

\textbf{Code.} \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_FRESH\_WEIGHTS=1} (steady\_newton.f90, measurement only)
and the band and ladder printout of \texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS} (one residual
evaluation per ladder length) were added; with neither key set the run is
unchanged (iteration 1 of the diagnostic run reproduces \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} = 1.945e-5).
The comment of \texttt{residual\_norms} (steady\_residual.f90) said the two regions\textquotesingle{}
rows are made dimensionless by different physics; \texttt{residual\_row\_scale} has
no region switch, and the comment now says so. The chains were not touched.

of sections 51 and 53 and of the handoff that the review showed to be

}
}

\subsection{55. One failure contract for the advection-derived product (2026-10-01)}\label{55-one-failure-contract-for-the-advection-derived-product-2026-10-01}

Plan \texttt{md/PLAN\_20260930\_rev3.md} A1, A2 and A6; the defect is finding 4.3 of
section 54 (a rejected column energy solve left its rows labeled corrected
and the evaluation exited 0). Implemented by the worker and checked on the
diff and the runs.

\textbf{The column solve} (\texttt{energy\_column\_newton}, \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90})
returns an outcome: \emph{rejected} with its reason (linear solve, nonfinite,
non-positive T, metal temperature band, iteration cap) and the triggering
cell, or \emph{step\_passed\_unverified} (the step test passed; no residual is
formed after the last step, so it is not called converged). A rejected
column keeps the marching profile and writes its WHOLE unknown set
\texttt{adv\_failed} (decision D2(i): no schema change; the ghost rows are written
failed but not counted). For a metal-band rejection the log prints the
triggering cell\textquotesingle s energy row term by term in erg cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1, at the column\textquotesingle s
T and at the run\textquotesingle s T (R = q0 fv/(mup mum dr\_step), the scaling of
\texttt{T\_equation}).

\textbf{The derived-state record.} \texttt{post\_process\_adv} returns \texttt{adv\_derived\_state}
(chemistry, column outcome and reason, physical-cell counts, \texttt{outer\ =\ unverified}: the outer loop is still ten fixed passes, \texttt{n\_pp\_passes}); both
callers print it and \texttt{write\_output} writes it as \texttt{\#\ adv\_derived\_state:\ ...}
into both \texttt{*\_adv} headers (schema and columns unchanged; the regression
comparison excludes \texttt{\#} lines). The evaluate route exits 7 when the record
is rejected (2 stays the certification refusal of the input state, written
first; 7 is used by nothing else in \texttt{src/} or the shell consumers).
\texttt{read\_adv\_validity} (\texttt{exhale\_transit\_lib.py}) refuses a row when
\texttt{adv\_T\_status\ /=\ 0} OR \texttt{adv\_comp\_status\ =\ 2}, returns \texttt{refused\_reason} and
\texttt{derived\_state} (None = unknown); \texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py} prints the record and
refuses a rejected \texttt{adv} spectrum unless \texttt{EXHALE\_TRANSIT\_DIAGNOSTIC=1}, which
writes the refusal into the spectrum metadata. \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh} requires the
binary, a fresh work directory, checks every step, records the md5 and
\texttt{state\_id} of both state files, the binary md5 and the \texttt{EXHALE\_*} overrides,
judges \texttt{solution} and \texttt{adv} separately, and states the EW in
percent-angstrom (the former \texttt{mA?} was wrong). \texttt{run\_case.sh} accepts 7 as
"solved state certified, adv product refused": the state is published with
its certification, the refusal and the verdict line go into \texttt{run.json}
(\texttt{adv\_product}, \texttt{adv\_derived\_state}), REPRODUCE.md and the last log line,
the transit pass is skipped (the transit tool itself exits 7 on a rejected
record) and earlier \texttt{tpm\_*} products of the case are moved to
\texttt{superseded\_case\_products/} so that none can be read as this run\textquotesingle s line
measure; an unknown exit still fails the pass. Launcher tests
(\texttt{LHS1140b/models/tests/}): \texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} 25/25 (the five exit-7
checks fail on the former tools), \texttt{runner\_records.sh} 18/18 after a fixture
defect of its own was fixed (its synthetic tree lacked
\texttt{he10830\_equivalent\_width.py} and the observation CSV that \texttt{status.py}
imports; 14/18 on the former fixture), and the other five launcher suites
unchanged.

\textbf{A6.} After every composition update (the H/He sweep, the metal re-solve
and its failure branch) \texttt{n\_tot}, \texttt{n\_e}, \texttt{mmw} and the caloric quantities are
refreshed, then \texttt{xion}, then heating, the energy solve and \texttt{p\_out}.
\texttt{wasp\_full} with \texttt{pp\_metals\ 2}: max \textbar p - (n\_tot + n\_e) k T\textbar/p of the written
composition 4.4e-8 -\textgreater{} 7.8e-16; the \texttt{\_adv} T column moves by 1.0e-6. The
\texttt{xion} refresh acts in every mode: on the fiducial atomic full-equation
state the outer passes now reach their fixed point (column iterations
20..4 -\textgreater{} 20..1 over the ten passes) and the \texttt{\_adv} T moves by 2.9e-10.
\textbf{Separate defect found and fixed:} the \texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} metal re-solve
never set \texttt{ieq\_cell\%T\_K}, so its charge exchange ran at the temperature of
the last equilibrium cell and the \texttt{cx\_require\_cell} guard aborted every
\texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} run (reproduced on \texttt{wasp\_full}; one line).

\textbf{The M2 rejection explained.} \texttt{ew\_M2\_s1} (molecular photochemical
fiducial, section 51): reason \texttt{metal\_T\_band} at cell 437 (r = 10.35 R\_p) on
10 of 10 passes: the column\textquotesingle s T 2445 K is 2.007 times the run\textquotesingle s 1218 K. The
row at 2445 K closes to 9.5e-12 of its largest term (compression work
5.6e-13 = photoheating 3.7e-13 + conduction 1.6e-13; radiative cooling
1e-16), and with that cell alone put back at 1218 K the conduction from its
neighbors (7.9e-10) leaves a residual equal to the largest term. So 2445 K
is a root of the corrected, conduction-coupled equation and only the band
refuses it; the band is a guard of the cell-by-cell Brent solve against
spurious hot metal-cooling roots and has no stated validity for the column
solve. Whether the column is to be accepted on its residual instead (plan
B2) is held for the user; the band is unchanged and the case stays
rejected (EW 1.1032 diagnostic; the \texttt{solution} spectrum 8.563 is a result).

\textbf{Checks.} \texttt{src/tests/adv\_static\_limit}: 53 -\textgreater{} 63 PASS, 0 FAIL, with the
gfortran and the ifx binary (rows K: a stored certified state evaluated
plain and with each of the five injected rejections,
\texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_TEST\_REJECT=\textless{}reason\textgreater{}}, measurement-only; rows L: the reader on
composition-only failure, retained/unsupported/not-evaluated kept apart, an
absent record read as unknown). Evaluate-only reruns in
\texttt{.P1/fiducial\_conduction/verify\_w1/}: M2 exit 7 with the record above;
fiducial atomic exit 0, \texttt{step\_passed\_unverified}, EW 0.6491 unchanged.
Known and left as it is: a gfortran runtime error exits 2, the code of the
certification refusal (TO\_BE\_DONE (o)).

\subsection{56. Continuation factors written exactly; legacy F7.5 tokens read as intervals (2026-10-01)}\label{56-continuation-factors-written-exactly-legacy-f75-tokens-read-as-intervals-2026-10-01}

Finding 5 of section 54 (plan A3). \texttt{load\_IC.f90} writes a factor s /= 1 of
\texttt{cond} and \texttt{interdiff\_enth} as \texttt{ES23.16E3} (\texttt{factor\_token\_text}, \texttt{adjustl};
s = 1 stays \texttt{T}), and the writer, reader, comparison and absent-token values
share one length, 32 (previously 8/16/16/8); with both factors the options
field is 296 of its 1024 characters, and a field or token that would not fit
is refused, never cut. A legacy F7.5 token (\texttt{factor\_token\_kind}, these two
tokens only) states the interval {[}v - 5e-6, v + 5e-6{]}: a requested factor
inside it loads the state as a seed with the claim dropped
(\texttt{legacy\_factor\_token}, one printed line, a \texttt{\#\ legacy\_factor\_token} history
line keeping the original text); a factor outside it is a physics change
under the existing rule (named on \texttt{Restart\ option\ change:}, or refused).
Malformed values are refused even when named. The run log
(\texttt{conduction\_scale}, \texttt{interdiffusion\_enthalpy\_scale}) and the setup report
print the factor \texttt{ES13.6} and the report names a migration. gfortran and
ifx write 0.773561, 1e-6, 1 - 1e-12 and 0 as the same token text and each
reloads the other\textquotesingle s state at the same factor. Test \texttt{restart\_factor}
(grid\_and\_gates 25): 38/38 PASS with both compilers (15 of the first 27
FAIL on 60a90fb); the gate tests of section 39 unchanged (gfortran 119
PASS, 11 XFAIL, 0 FAIL; the 16 ifx FAILs are the fixtures\textquotesingle{} 1-ulp compiler
difference, present before). \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/run.sh} uses \texttt{-module} and
\texttt{-qmkl} for ifx (before, ifx could not link the Fortran drivers). Round
trip: rho, v, p bitwise (gfortran) or 1 ulp (ifx), T and species 1-3 ulp,
the loader\textquotesingle s own arithmetic. States written on 2026-09-29/30 with F7.5
tokens (every chain rung) keep them and load only as seeds.

\subsection{57. The H2 conductivity without overflow (2026-10-01)}\label{57-the-h2-conductivity-without-overflow-2026-10-01}

Finding 10.2 of section 54 (plan A4). \texttt{h2\_conductivity} evaluated
E(x) = x\^{}2 e\^{}x/(e\^{}x - 1)\^{}2 (x = 5987 K/T) as written: (e\^{}x - 1)\^{}2 overflows
above x \textasciitilde{} 355 (T \textless{} 16.9 K), where the form returned 0 in place of
E \textless{} 1e-148, and x\^{}2 e\^{}x above x = 696.69, so 8.553 K \textless= T \textless{} 8.593 K gave
NaN, and \texttt{thermal\_conductivity} added n\_H2 x NaN even where n\_H2 = 0. (The
review and section 54 had said "NaN above x \textasciitilde{} 355"; the NaN interval is the
narrow one.) E is now \texttt{einstein\_heat\_capacity}: x\^{}2 e\^{}-x/(1 - e\^{}-x)\^{}2 for
x \textgreater= 0.05, the series 1 - x\^{}2/12 + x\^{}4/240 - x\^{}6/6048 below (truncation
\textless{} 2.3e-16; the two agree at the switch to 6.7e-16), 0 above x = 700;
\texttt{h2\_conductivity} stops on a non-positive or non-finite T; the H2 term is
formed only where n\_H2 \textgreater{} 0, in the same summation order, so an atomic
mixture is bitwise unchanged. Fit and range (200-2000 K) unchanged.
\texttt{src/tests/energy\_update}: 54 -\textgreater{} 69 PASS (8.57 K = 1.3235866213750098e3,
200/748/2000 K unchanged to 1e-14, the 1e6 K series limit, continuity,
n\_H2 = 0 mixture bitwise atomic). Stationary evaluate of the molecular
scalar He/H 1.45 conduction state, HEAD against HEAD + fix (ifx):
\texttt{Hydro\_ioniz} and \texttt{Ion\_species} bitwise; \texttt{*\_adv} at rounding (T 7e-14).

\subsection{58. The energy-row floor was a rounding staircase of the absorbed fraction (2026-10-01)}\label{58-the-energy-row-floor-was-a-rounding-staircase-of-the-absorbed-fraction-2026-10-01}

Plan C1-C3, on the state of section 53 (\texttt{diag\_R/floor\_jvadd}). Keys added,
default off: \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_DUMP\_CELL=\textless{}j\textgreater{}} writes every He 2\^{}3S-branch solve of
cell j to \texttt{ieq\_cell\_dump.txt} (tags, T, densities, every rate coefficient,
start and returned vectors, dimensional rows, turnover bounds, normalized
residual, the forward-difference Jacobian) and the cell\textquotesingle s photoionization
rates at perturbed entry states to \texttt{ieq\_cell\_rate\_probe.txt};
\texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_TRACE\_SEED=shift\textbar{}h2\textbar{}partition} selects seed B of the
two-seed trace (\texttt{partition}: the elemental inventory and the charge closure
kept, a fraction of H I and He I moved into H II and He II). A5:
\texttt{report\_accepted\_cell\_state} names unknown 4 by the system (He 2\^{}3S in the
atomic triplet branch). With every key unset the outputs equal the HEAD
build\textquotesingle s.

\textbf{Verdict: hypothesis (b) of section 53, and the cause.} At cell 307 the
elimination alternated between x(H+) = 0.50307588606921 and
0.50307588605993 (1.8e-11 apart). The dumped rows are reproduced from the
dumped coefficients in binary64 (146 records, to 1.8e-17 of the turnover
bound). Each returned x is the 50-digit root of ITS OWN pass\textquotesingle s coefficients
to 1 ulp and 1.4e-11 (normalized) from the other\textquotesingle s; the binary64 residual is
monotone across 41 ulps, not flat. The coefficients differ because P\_HI,
the cell\textquotesingle s photoionization rate, is a staircase function of the cell\textquotesingle s own
entry n(H I): steps of 4-5e-11 relative every 1.7e-11 in n(H I).
Cause: \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_per\_unit\_depth} (\texttt{utilities.f90}) took the closed
form (1 - e\^{}-d)/d for d \textgreater= 1e-8, whose rounding is a staircase with
relative steps eps/d (1.5e-9 at this cell\textquotesingle s d = 1.5e-7). The smooth
composition map contracts (-0.073 per pass, measured with the partition
seed), but its fixed point lay on a step, so a period-2 orbit resulted, and
that orbit was the floor: two seeds end on it in opposite phase,
\textbar\textbar R\_B - R\_A\textbar\textbar{} = 1.7e-5. The "flat residual" of section 53 is withdrawn.

\textbf{Fix.} The Taylor series through d\^{}8 (Horner) for 1e-8 \textless= d \textless{} 1e-2
(truncation \textless{} 3e-25), the closed form above (error \textless= 2.2e-14); the old
comment\textquotesingle s truncation figure for the three-term series at 1e-8 was 4e-25 and
is 4.2e-26. Kahan\textquotesingle s (1 - u)/(-ln u) does not work: ifx -O3 -xHost folds
ln(exp(-d)) to -d and the output was bitwise the closed form\textquotesingle s. Reach: every
XUV photoionization and photoheating rate (\texttt{photoionization\_field\_at\_cell\_H}
and \texttt{\_HHe} through \texttt{cell\_mean\_attenuation}, the a\_tau = 0 branch) and the
H2O/OH FUV band rates. Effect on that state (max over
physical cells, new against the control): x(H+) 7.8e-11, T 1.3e-11, rho
1.6e-10, v 2.2e-6 at the base cell only (below 1e-9 elsewhere), the mean
rho v r\^{}2 over r \textgreater= 1.1 R\_p 2.7e-10.

\textbf{Result.} floor\_jvadd with the chain settings (\texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_SER=2}, cap
400, omega 1, factor 0.773561): ONE outer pass, 2 JFNK steps, info = 0,
CERTIFIED, energy row 1.16e-7 (tolerance 1e-6), 1.3 s; the HEAD build with
the same settings: 400 iterations, info = 1, energy row 3.56e-5, still
9.0e-6 after three passes, never certified. Test \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_switch}
(physics\_probe 28): 14 assertions (quad-precision error on each side,
continuity at both switches, mpmath at 1.5e-7, 1e-3, 0.1, 1, 10, no
staircase over 41 points at 1.5e-7), all pass with gfortran and ifx at -O0;
the former form fails 5. Runs in \texttt{diag\_R/w4\_*}; figures in
\texttt{diag\_R/w4\_figures/}.

Consequence for the chains (stopped 2026-10-01, section 55 of the handoff):
their slow rungs were this staircase, not the acceptance rule or the
stopping measure of the elimination (plan C4, which stays a comment defect
of that measure, not a cure).

\subsection{59. One (1 - e\^{}-x)/x for the code, and the other small-value forms (2026-10-01)}\label{59-one-1---e-xx-for-the-code-and-the-other-small-value-forms-2026-10-01}

\texttt{one\_minus\_exp\_over\_x} (\texttt{utilities.f90}: the series through x\^{}8 below 1e-2,
the closed form above, error \textless= 2.2e-14) is now the only evaluation of the
quotient. Evaluated through it, each with its own name, limits and meaning:
\texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_per\_unit\_depth} (section 58), \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_over\_tau}
and \texttt{absorbed\_photon\_fraction} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}: the case-A/B ground
capture weights and the on-the-spot absorption of recombination radiation;
former switch 1e-4 with errors 2.2e-12 and 4e-14), the thin branch of
\texttt{line\_escape\_probability\_one\_face} (\texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}, the metal and helium
fine-structure line escape; former closed form from 1e-8, error up to
2e-8), and the Ly-alpha Sobolev escape \texttt{beta\_sob} (\texttt{lya\_rt.f90}), which
returned 0 in place of 1 for tau\_S below \textasciitilde1e-16 and at tau\_S = 0 (a limit
defect; reached only with excited hydrogen on, which the LHS 1140 b runs do
not have). Then the three forms that lose accuracy where their value is
small: the stimulated-emission factor 1 - e\^{}-Ek/T of
\texttt{line\_center\_opacity\_lte} and the absorbed Lyman-continuum fraction of
\texttt{excited\_hydrogen.f90} as y (1 - e\^{}-y)/y (limit y), and the lower
incomplete gamma functions of the l-mixing rate, \texttt{lower\_gamma\_2} and
\texttt{lower\_gamma\_3}, as their power series in Horner form below 0.25 (through
x\^{}12) and 0.5 (through x\^{}17), closed form above, errors \textless= 1.1e-14 (the
former three-term series switched at 1e-2 with jumps of 6.7e-8 and 8.4e-8
there). \texttt{fine\_structure\_escape\_probability} takes its thin reference in
quadruple precision (its double reference was the cancelling form).
Test \texttt{one\_minus\_exp\_over\_x\_sites} (physics\_probe 46): 87 assertions
(mpmath values, continuity at every switch, no staircase over 41-point
scans, the limits), all pass with gfortran and ifx at -O0; the former code
fails 36. Evaluate-only: floor\_jvadd moves \textless= 5.7e-14 beyond section 58;
the molecular photochemical s1 state \textless= 1.2e-10 (H II, O II at a ghost
cell), CERTIFIED before and after (mass 2.7e-9, energy 2.3e-8); the last
three forms change neither run (excited hydrogen off; y of order 0.1-1 on
the closed-form side).

\subsection{61. The column energy solve accepted on its residual; the refused rows carry the solution (2026-10-01)}\label{61-the-column-energy-solve-accepted-on-its-residual-the-refused-rows-carry-the-solution-2026-10-01}

User decision of 2026-10-01 ("잔차"): the conduction-coupled column of the
advected profile is judged on its residual, not on the metal temperature
band (TO\_BE\_DONE (p), plan rev3 B2). Implemented by the worker of section
55 and checked on the diff, the tests and the three evaluate-only runs.

\textbf{Acceptance.} After the iteration stops (step 1e-10 or 100 iterations, a
stopping rule only) the transport terms are formed again at the final
profile, and every unknown\textquotesingle s energy row R = q0 fv/(mup mum dr\_step) must
satisfy \textbar R\textbar{} \textless= atol\_E + cert\_tol\_energy S\_E, with S\_E the largest of the
grouped terms (div(rho e v) as one term, p div v, heating, cooling, the
conduction divergence, the element and the carrier enthalpy divergences)
and atol\_E = 10 eps times the parts the row is assembled from (the grouped
terms, the two advective halves, the three conduction face terms). MEASURED:
the stagnated floor is 0.73 to 0.99 eps of that sum on the three conduction
states, while the grouped sum alone under-states it by 1e3 to 1e5, the
conduction divergence being a small difference of three large face terms.
Today the absolute term never decides: the achieved \textbar R\textbar{} is 1e4 below
rtol S\_E. The {[}0.5, 2{]} T\_in band is removed from the column (it guards the
cell-by-cell Brent solve, which keeps it, and had refused a root, section
55). Outcome \texttt{converged\_on\_residual}; reason \texttt{residual\_above\_tolerance}
with the worst cell\textquotesingle s term table; record fields \texttt{column\_worst\_cell},
\texttt{column\_worst\_residual}, \texttt{column\_worst\_residual\_rel}.

\textbf{Cells without a marching root.} Cells whose marching Brent step finds no
root (its bracket {[}0.05, 4{]} T\_in) are column unknowns by default and the
residual decides (\texttt{cells\_marching\_unrooted} in the record;
\texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_COLUMN\_EXCLUDE\_UNROOTED=1} restores the old set, measurement
only). On the photochemical s1 state three cells at 24.5-28.5 R\_p have
their roots at 4.0-4.3 T\_in; with them in the column every pass solves the
same set (the 354/351 alternation is gone).

\textbf{Refused rows carry the solution.} The status-3 rows of that product
(1.0-1.09 R\_p) were T-unsupported but composition-corrected, and their
corrected n(He 2\^{}3S) was 70 to 5600 times the run\textquotesingle s: the molecule-free
advection systems omit the He(2\^{}3S) + H2 destruction that the solution\textquotesingle s
balance includes. A composition class (formed with the particle-count class
on the state handed in, no threshold) now refuses a cell whose H+, He+ or
He(2\^{}3S) loses faster to H2 (R10 + R13, R17 + R23, \texttt{ioniz\_HeI23S\_H2}, from
\texttt{mol\_rates}) than to the carried channels; the class is applied inside the
passes, so the cascade takes the run\textquotesingle s composition as its history there;
the metal re-solve of \texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} skips refused cells; and every refused
row is written with the run\textquotesingle s exact numbers (species and metal stages when
the composition field is refused; T when the T field is 1, 3 or 4; p, heat,
cool when both are refused): the restored fields equal the solution\textquotesingle s
columns to 0 relative on the three states; a row with corrected T and
refused composition is not the solution as a whole. The class is a
threshold rule on the input state and does not bound the omitted H2
contribution. The transit census still counts
them as refused (173 rows to 1.089 R\_p, photochemical; 246 rows to 1.322
R\_p, scalar He/H 1.45).

\textbf{Results} (evaluate-only, \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh}, tree ifx binary; EW in
percent-angstrom, adv / solution):

\textbar{} state \textbar{} column \textbar{} unknowns (unrooted) \textbar{} worst \textbar R\textbar/S\_E \textbar{} EW adv \textbar{} EW solution \textbar{}
\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar{}
\textbar{} fiducial atomic full-equation \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 500 (0) \textbar{} 2.8e-11 \textbar{} 0.6491 \textbar{} 7.5179 \textbar{}
\textbar{} molecular photochemical He/H 9, conduction \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 329 (3) \textbar{} 1.6e-11 \textbar{} \textbf{0.7705} \textbar{} 8.5630 \textbar{}
\textbar{} molecular scalar He/H 1.45, conduction \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 256 (0) \textbar{} 2.9e-12 \textbar{} \textbf{0.1408} \textbar{} 2.1471 \textbar{}

All three exit 0 with the COLUMN accepted; the derived state as a whole
stays \texttt{unverified}: the transport coefficients are formed on the input
composition (plan B1), the outer iteration is the fixed ten passes (B3)
and the momentum balance is not measured (B4). The
photochemical adv EW of an ACCEPTED corrected profile is 0.7705 against
1.1032 of the refused marching profile (section 51, now superseded) and
the observed 1.108 +- 0.029: 30 per cent low. Its He 10830 line-center
optical depth peaks at 0.59 at b = 1.2 R\_p (the solution\textquotesingle s at 1.45), 85 per
cent of it from 1.1-3 R\_p, where T\_adv/T\_run is 1.00-1.04 and n(He 2\^{}3S)
is 0.93 to 0.60 of the solution\textquotesingle s (the advected, non-equilibrium
population); T\_adv reaches 2 to 4.4 T\_run beyond 10 R\_p, which carries
under 1 per cent of tau. The correction of the scalar He/H 1.45 product
moved from 0.1525 to 0.1408 (-7.7 per cent) because its composition class
reaches 1.32 R\_p. Tests: \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 62 PASS (63 before: rows K
have five reasons now), \texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} 25/25.

\subsection{62. The Codex review of the 2026-10-01 summary, applied (2026-10-01)}\label{62-the-codex-review-of-the-2026-10-01-summary-applied-2026-10-01}

\texttt{md/code\_improvements\_20261001.md} summarizes sections 53-61; Codex
reviewed it in place (its comments R1-R8 stay in that file). Its
statements were checked against the tree before anything was changed.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  R2, R5, R8 (wording): "exact root" is agreement to floating-point
  accuracy (0.65 and 1.64 ulp on the last two records; the two x(H+) are
  9.2e-12 apart, 1.8e-11 relative); refusal of an \texttt{\_adv} row is per field
  (composition, or T), so a row with corrected T and refused composition is
  not the solution as a whole, and the H2 class is a threshold rule that
  does not bound the omitted contribution; "profiles move by \textless= 1e-10" was
  false as a general statement: on the diagnostic state the maximum
  relative change over physical cells is x(H+) 7.8e-11, T 1.3e-11, rho
  1.6e-10, v 2.2e-6 (the base cell only), the mean rho v r\^{}2 over
  r \textgreater= 1.1 R\_p 2.7e-10. The summary, sections 58, 60 and 61 and the
  handoff now say so.
\item
  R3 (HIGH, correct): what section 61 accepts is the column subproblem
  with lagged coefficients (the transport coefficients on the input
  composition, the fine-structure escape probabilities of the start of the
  pass); the full-state energy residual on one derived composition (plan
  B1), the outer iteration (B3) and the momentum balance (B4) are not
  tested. The stale comments of \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90} (the derived-state
  block) and of \texttt{adv\_derived\_state\_verdict} (\texttt{exhale\_transit\_lib.py}),
  which still said the column residual is untested, now list what is and
  is not tested.
\item
  R4 (MEDIUM, correct): \texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py} continued on an \texttt{unknown}
  derived-state verdict (no record) with a warning; it now exits 7 unless
  \texttt{EXHALE\_TRANSIT\_DIAGNOSTIC=1}, as for a rejected record, and the
  metadata carries \texttt{adv\_derived\_state\_unknown} and the override.
  \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh} passes the key on an unknown record in its diagnostic
  mode; the manual and the READMEs say so. adv\_static\_limit rows M (the
  rows-K product with its record stripped): refused without the key, a
  curve with the key stating the unknown record; 64 PASS (62 + 2).
  Left as it is: \texttt{run\_case.sh} calls the transit without the key, so a
  product written by a binary older than the record would fail its case;
  the current binary always writes the record.
\item
  R6 (correct): the two bitwise rows of \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_switch}
  (H2O and OH band rates against the XUV cell mean) fail under ifx -O3
  -xHost by 1 ulp because the optimizer reassociates the test\textquotesingle s reference
  product; they now assert 2 ulps (\texttt{...\_to\_2ulp}, 4.5e-16) with a
  DIAGNOSTIC line of the bitwise-unequal counts: 14/14 with the suite\textquotesingle s
  -O0 flags, with gfortran -O3 (0 unequal) and with ifx -O3 -xHost (552
  and 405 unequal of 1506, max 2.2e-16). A second copy of the function
  would still be caught (its difference is the closed form\textquotesingle s 1e-9 to
  1e-14 error below d = 1e-2).
\item
  R7: the saved EWs reintegrate to 0.6490803760 / 7.5179432737,
  0.7705246807 / 8.5629850578, 0.1407893262 / 2.1470782882 (adv /
  solution); recorded in the summary.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{63. The reviewed part of the LHS 1140 b EW worktree, taken into the tree (2026-10-03)}\label{63-the-reviewed-part-of-the-lhs-1140-b-ew-worktree-taken-into-the-tree-2026-10-03}

\texttt{md/lhs1140b\_ew\_codex\_review\_20261003.md} reviews the changes an autonomous
Codex run made in the detached worktree \texttt{../LHS1140b\_EW\_20261001/code}
(same base, 3152b74). Its sections 3.1 and 3.2 (accept; fix first) and 4
(a defect of this tree) are applied here; section 3.3 (molecular-ion
transport, joint species-flux modules, metastable balance diagnostic,
HeH+ Newton rows) is not. Each item was re-derived against this tree\textquotesingle s
code, not copied.

\textbf{Physics corrected.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Recombination photons absorbed by H2 (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}
  \texttt{recombination\_radiation\_absorbed}, \texttt{ionization\_equilibrium.f90}): the
  whole H2 share made H2+ and heated with one threshold. It is now split
  into the four final states of \texttt{h2\_photo\_channels} (M H2+, S dissociative
  ionization, D double ionization, N neutral dissociation) in the ratio of
  the channel cross sections the stellar field uses
  (\texttt{h2\_photoabsorption\_cross\_sections}, shared with \texttt{set\_energy\_vectors}),
  total absorption unchanged, heat = photoelectron(s) + fragment kinetic
  energy of the channel. The capture kinetic energy of a continuum channel
  goes to every final state (energy conservation; the Codex version gave it
  to the ionizing ones only). Channel shares at the default switches,
  MEASURED: S 1.9-6.3\% at 19.8-31.1 eV; S 11.4\% and N 1.1\% at 40.8 eV
  (He II Ly-alpha, inside the neutral window the old comment said it was
  outside of); S 17.0\% and D 0.37\% at 54.4 eV.
\item
  \texttt{h2\_channel\_threshold} of N: 33 -\textgreater{} 32 eV, the lower continuity anchor
  where \texttt{frac\_H2\_neutral\_dissociation} turns on (32-33 eV absorptions had a
  cross section and no energy recipient). No current recombination channel
  lies in 32-33 eV.
\item
  The recombination-coupling condition at the two sweep sites is
  \texttt{use\_h\_rec\_escape\ .or.\ (use\_he\_rec\_coupling\ .and.\ thereis\_He)}; the old
  form could read the \texttt{\_hrc} arrays unset in a helium-free run.
\item
  Carrier enthalpy flux (\texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90}
  \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_face\_flux}): the recoil set of the energy row is now the
  set \texttt{carrier\_write\_back} rescales. Before, H+ took part of the hydrogen
  recoil although a carried H+ (\texttt{Ionization\ transport:\ True}) is written from
  its own row, and HeH+ took part although the write-back never moves it.
  Physics document \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/molecules\_metals.tex} updated.
\item
  Class 6 of the ionization sweep: a constrained projection that fails
  \texttt{ionization\_fractions\_physical} keeps the entry composition and records
  the element-budget violation (it was accepted as a root); the class-6
  residual is that of the retained state (also stated in
  \texttt{steady\_newton.f90}). \texttt{impose\_transported\_fractions} is applied before the
  admissibility test and at write-back. The decay-rate diagnostic releases
  the He II / He III fixed flags as well.
\item
  Jeans escape flux of \texttt{collisional\_validity.py}: n vbar/4 (1+lambda)e\^{}-lambda;
  the old prefactor was 2/sqrt(pi) = 1.128 too large. The planet radius and
  mass of a run are read from its \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} by one rule in
  \texttt{examples/exhale\_io.py} \texttt{load\_run} (the lower-atmosphere profile and
  \texttt{base.inp} overwrite R0); \texttt{collisional\_validity.py} and
  \texttt{formation\_energy\_flux\_diagnostic.py} use it. He mass 3.9715 m\_H (the
  table\textquotesingle s), not 4.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Restart and output.}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Exact code-unit restart: \texttt{conserved\_state.txt} (new
  \texttt{conserved\_state\_restart.f90}) beside every \textquotesingle eq\textquotesingle{} write and every pass-state
  generation, read as \texttt{conserved\_state\_IC.txt} when it belongs to the IC pair
  (checksums, grid, species, boundary). A state that cannot be written
  exactly is not written (warning, existing file removed); a file that does
  not match, an option-change restart and a seed run use the dimensional pair
  with a notice. Exit statuses unchanged. README, manual (\texttt{conserved\_state.txt}
  subsection), \texttt{README\_HOWTO.md} pass-state restore, \texttt{md/input\_schema.md},
  GUI copy, mapper refusal and \texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py} (post-processing
  step = \texttt{Restart\ intent:\ stationary\ evaluate}) follow.
\item
  Species densities re-formed after \texttt{Apply\_BC} at the six output and
  evaluation sites (the Newton residual\textquotesingle s order); ghost output rows formed
  from the conserved ghost cells by the tree\textquotesingle s own routines
  (\texttt{boundary\_gas\_state\_from\_conserved}); \texttt{write\_resolved\_config} after the
  certification on both branches of the loaded-restart route.
\item
  Comments corrected: subsonic outflow ghost (one incoming characteristic),
  ion-neutral friction (Langevin friction independent of T, rigid core
  as T\^{}(1/2); \texttt{1/D\ =\ 1/D\_pol\ +\ 1/D\_hs} stated as an interpolation, up to a
  factor 2 in friction at the crossover), the Banks \& Kockarts diameter
  (2.99 A, not 2.7 A; also \texttt{md/binary\_diffusion\_design.md}), mol\_rates R14
  and R19 sources (coefficients unchanged), \texttt{n\_car\_max} derived from the
  species table.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Not taken, with reason.} The element eddy-flux outputs (no caller once
the row dumps of 3.3 are left out); the \texttt{spherical\_flux\_divergence}
refactor (stops on a NaN trial; the loop stays); the Codex wording that
weakened two correct statements of \texttt{collisional\_validity}.

\textbf{Tests.} Four builds from one source (\texttt{3152b74}; all changes; the
enthalpy recoil set only; the recombination channels and chemistry only),
run single-threaded on copies of \texttt{hp\_front} (100 steps) and
\texttt{carrier\_model\_a\_newton} (stationary restart), MEASURED largest relative
change over physical cells against the control build:

\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot

hp\_front & recombination channels & H2+ 1.1e-4, H3+ 7.0e-6, HeH+ 8.8e-7, cool 7.5e-7 \\

}

Both are snapshots, not converged states, so these are the changes of one

H3+, HeH+, He 2\^{}3S) overlaid and inspected (session scratch
\texttt{cmp/cmp\_*.pdf}).

\texttt{make\ test} on the merged tree (\texttt{EXHALE.x} 60bd2895135134813116b4b0ac63fcaa):
44 of 48 entries PASS; \texttt{steady\_completion\_flag} and
\texttt{steady\_selfconsistent\_residual} fail without \texttt{EXHALE\_STEADY\_RUNLOGS} (they
read the logs of a steady run and have no default; not a code result);
\texttt{boundary\_state} and \texttt{physics\_probe} failed on the test defects below and
pass after them (\texttt{physics\_probe} 2295 PASS, 0 FAIL). \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux}
gains section (e): a column with H+ (a third of the atomic hydrogen) and

local and with H+ carried; both rows pass (1.8e-15) and both FAIL on the

asserted a bitwise zero face-state gap, which the density re-formation
after \texttt{Apply\_BC} moves to rounding (MEASURED 1.6e-15; the entry-composition
order it guards against leaves 7.5e-7), now a 1e-13 relative bound.
The exact restart was exercised on \texttt{wasp\_he23off} inputs: two write-reload
round trips bitwise in u and f\_sp; a negative ghost fraction is not written
and the run exits 0; stale and mismatched files are ignored with a notice.
Not run: molecular-seed, pass-state generation, the closure driver, GUI.

\textbf{Open, for the user.} R19 (HeH+ + H -\textgreater{} H2+ + He): 9.1e-10 cm\^{}3 s\^{}-1 is a
room-temperature measurement; De Fazio et al. (2014) Table 2 gives 1.54e-9
(1000 K) and 1.65e-9 (2000 K), Esposito (2015) via Sil et al. (2025)
1.82e-9 exp(-121/T); not changed. Esposito, Coppola \& De Fazio (2015) was
then read in its accepted manuscript (the published version could not be
obtained): a Boltzmann average over all 178 rovibrational states of HeH+,
10-15000 K, with the authors\textquotesingle{} own polynomial fit (1.52e-9 at 1000 K, a
maximum 2.00e-9 near 6000 K; 1-6\% from De Fazio up to 2000 K; the Sil
Arrhenius form is 9-11\% below it at 4000-8000 K). The R19 comment of
\texttt{mol\_rates.f90} now states this. The constant is kept because it is the
Koskinen et al. (2022) Table 1 entry the Model A comparison uses; the
replacement is the user\textquotesingle s decision (decided the same day: section 64). \texttt{python/paper\_data.py} still reads the
input.inp planet radius.

\subsection{64. R19 is the thermal rate of Esposito, Coppola \& De Fazio (2015) (2026-10-03)}\label{64-r19-is-the-thermal-rate-of-esposito-coppola--de-fazio-2015-2026-10-03}

HeH+ + H -\textgreater{} H2+ + He (\texttt{mol\_rates.f90} \texttt{rk\_R19\_HeHp\_H}) was the Koskinen et
al. (2022) Table 1 constant 9.1e-10 cm\^{}3 s\^{}-1, a measurement near 300 K
(Karpas et al. 1979) held at every wind temperature. By the user\textquotesingle s decision
of 2026-10-03 it is now the thermal rate of Esposito, Coppola \& De Fazio
(2015, J. Phys. Chem. A 119, 12615), read in the accepted manuscript (the
published version could not be obtained): close-coupling cross sections for
v = 0, j = 0-5 and quasi-classical trajectories for the other states,
Boltzmann-averaged over all 178 rovibrational states of HeH+, in the
authors\textquotesingle{} fit k = sum\_\{i=0\}\^{}\{6\} a\_i (log10 T)\^{}i (max error 2.5\%; their eq 4
prints the upper limit 7 with seven coefficients, and the seven reproduce
their Fig. 6). T is held to the authors\textquotesingle{} 10-15000 K (the polynomial is
negative below about 6 K and above about 3e4 K). The function now takes T;
its two callers (\texttt{System\_HeH\_mol.f90} \texttt{set\_mol\_coeffs},
\texttt{molecular\_reaction\_heat.f90}) pass the temperature they already pass to
the other rates.

MEASURED values of the built function: 1.088e-9 (300 K), 1.524e-9
(1000 K), 1.753e-9 (2000 K), 1.997e-9 (5354 K), 2.004e-9 (6000 K), held
1.571e-9 above 15000 K and 5.25e-11 below 10 K; 1-6\% from De Fazio (2014)
Table 2 at 100-2000 K, within 3\% of Garcia Munoz (2025) Table A.6 at
500-5000 K. The Sil et al. (2025) Arrhenius form of the same rate is not
used (9-11\% below it at 4000-8000 K).

Effect, MEASURED against the build of section 63 (same source but R19),
single-threaded, largest relative change over physical cells; full profiles
of T, v, rho, H2, H+, H2+, H3+, HeH+ and He+ overlaid and inspected
(session scratch \texttt{cmp/r19\_*.pdf}):

\subsection{65. The Codex review of sections 63-64, checked (2026-10-03)}\label{65-the-codex-review-of-sections-63-64-checked-2026-10-03}

Codex reviewed sections 63-64 and the documents of the day in
\texttt{md/claude\_code\_physics\_review\_20261003.md} (with \texttt{\_checks.json},
\texttt{\_timescales.json}, \texttt{\_profiles.pdf}, \texttt{\_carrier\_test.txt}, \texttt{\_r19\_probe.txt})
and changed five files (\texttt{examples/exhale\_io.py}, the two md documents,
\texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py} and the scattering assessment). Each
change was checked against the code.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \texttt{examples/exhale\_io.py} \texttt{load\_run}: \texttt{EXHALE\_resolved.out} is now read from
  the parent of the output directory, not from the directory of the input
  file. Correct: EXHALE writes the resolved file in the run directory and
  the outputs in its \texttt{output/}, so an input file from elsewhere could pair
  an output with another run\textquotesingle s radius and mass (Codex reproduced a factor 2
  in Mdot/M\_p with a foreign file). Exercised on \texttt{lower\_profile} with and
  without an input file: both read that run\textquotesingle s own resolved file.
\item
  \texttt{md/binary\_diffusion\_design.md}, \texttt{md/collisional\_validity.md},
  \texttt{src/utils/collisional\_validity.py}: HeH+ is stated not to be a friction
  carrier of the element operator (true: \texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90}
  leaves it out of both carrier lists), and the "omitted channels are
  conservative" statement is limited to the retained coefficients. Both
  correct.
\item
  \texttt{md/lhs1140b\_ew\_codex\_scattering\_assessment\_20261003.md}: Codex added a
  qualification. Its substance was right: the lifetime had been compared
  with bulk advection only. The diffusion and drift times are now MEASURED
  on the same state (HeH+ diffusion by Blanc\textquotesingle s law from the pair
  coefficients of \texttt{collisional\_validity.py}, K\_zz = 1e9, settling in
  gravity and the ambipolar field eE = -kT d ln(n\_e T)/dr plus gradient
  drift, n\_e the production charge budget): at the He 2\^{}3S column peak
  t\_chem = 11 s against t\_adv 2.8e5, t\_diff 4.9e7 and t\_drift 1.2e7 s, so
  Da stays 2.5e4, also with every Coulomb partner dropped (D 30 times
  larger; t\_drift 3.9e5 s). Advection is the shortest transport time except
  in 210 cells of the slow base layer below 1.18 R\_p (diffusion shorter;
  0.4\% of the radial He 2\^{}3S column). The qualification is replaced by these
  numbers and the overstated sentences are corrected.
  Two Codex follow-ups in the same review file then showed that the
  ambipolar field adds to gravity in 197 of the 500 cells (a field-free
  drift is not an upper bound; eE/mg down to -0.92 with the metal-ion
  electrons, -0.49 without), that the neutral-only row is a sensitivity and
  not a bound on the pair law, that the opening claim was too categorical,
  and that the first field-inclusive table still carried field-free drift
  times and a wrong count ("advection shortest in all but one cell", which
  had missed the cells where diffusion is shortest). All were reproduced and
  corrected; the assessment states the field, the margin at the peak (D 43
  times larger for drift to reach advection, 1.1e6 times for it to reach
  chemistry), the local scope of the claim, and that the column shares are
  radial, not EW weights. Da at the column peak is 2.5e4 throughout.
\end{itemize}

Points of the review that stand as open items: the recombination
photoelectrons of \texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90} heat locally with no secondary
ionization or excitation, where the stellar path partitions the
photoelectron energy (stated at the code site; at 54.4 eV an H2 channel
leaves a photoelectron above the secondary-ionization threshold; effect not
measured); and the thermal R19 rate assumes HeH+ internal states in
equilibrium, which chemical relaxation does not show.

\subsection{66. The LHS 1140 b EW plan started: phases 0 and 1 (2026-10-03)}\label{66-the-lhs-1140-b-ew-plan-started-phases-0-and-1-2026-10-03}

The plan \texttt{md/PLAN\_20261003\_lhs1140b\_ew.md} was approved by the user with
all five decisions of its section 3. Runs are in
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/}; the binary is
\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_f036290f.x} (ifx, clean export of dd4a591,
manifest \texttt{.P2/BINARY\_MANIFEST\_f036290f65fd.txt}).

\textbf{Phase 0} (\texttt{.P2/ew\_fit/NOTES\_phase0.md}).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Entry \texttt{molecular\_photochem\_gj1132\_kzzprofile\_HeH2.09.entry/} from the
  certified generation g0001 (2026-09-29) of that case; its input differs
  from the He/H 9 entry in the planet name and He/H only.
\item
  Evaluate-only on f036290f: both the HeH2.09 state and the He/H 9
  conduction state are NOT CERTIFIED. HeH2.09: cell-1 rows O(1), from the
  base being rebuilt as \texttt{discrete\_equilibrium\_v5} from a state written
  under v4 (the He/H 9 entry was in the same position and certified through
  its conduction chain). He/H 9 conduction (v5 already): carrier H2 0.838,
  energy 2.6e-2, from the equation changes of sections 63-64.
\item
  The stored EWs reproduce bitwise on their own binaries: 0.7705246807
  (He/H 9 conduction, \texttt{verify\_w1d/EXHALE\_w1d.x}) and 1.5008683274
  (HeH2.09, \texttt{EXHALE\_6dd54955.x}). On f036290f (diagnostic, refused state)
  they are 0.7705418877 and 1.4601886445.
\item
  Conduction continuation of HeH2.09 started on lart4 15:33 KST
  (\texttt{.P2/ew\_fit/factor\_continuation\_chain.sh}, a copy writing into
  \texttt{.P2/ew\_fit}, with \texttt{config\_rung.sh} requiring BIN); rung 1, s = 0.005,
  certified in 21 passes.

\textbf{Phase 1} (\texttt{.P2/ew\_fit/phase1/NOTES\_phase1.md}; all on the He/H 9
conduction state, a solution of the w1d equations, each number with its
binary).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Where the line forms (EW with n(2\^{}3S) zeroed beyond r\_cut): on the
  local-balance solution state (EW 8.563) 17\% inside 5 R\_p and 18\% beyond
  13 R\_p; on the advected product of the same state (EW 0.7705, MEASURED
  here, \texttt{phase1/ew\_adv/truncation\_adv.txt}) 17\% inside 2 R\_p, 55\% inside
  5 R\_p, 77\% inside 8 R\_p, 95\% inside 13 R\_p. Kn\_bulk = 0.1 at 6.97 R\_p.
  The solution and advected EWs of one state differ by a factor 11: carried
  ionization is a first-order effect on the EW.
\item
  He 2\^{}3S local balance (solution state): first-order EW change +0.013 to
  +0.019 percent-angstrom (2.2-3.3 times the numerical target 0.0058).
\item
  Ion-neutral drift: 0.3-1.1 of the element velocity at 3-30 R\_p (H+/H I
  and He+/He I; the resonant charge-exchange cross section used in one
  bracket is not verified in a published source). The one-velocity stage
  flux is not valid there.
\item
  Stage enthalpy (\texttt{TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (j)): up to 2e-2 of the energy-row scale;
  required whenever ionization transport is on.
\item
  Recombination photoelectrons above 10.2 eV deposited as heat: below 5e-5
  of the heating, but up to 43 times the energy-row tolerance at 1.0-1.5
  R\_p; \textless{} 2.3e-7 of the row scale where the line forms.
\item
  Energy coupling to Photochem: the wind conducts 4.25 erg cm\^{}-2 s\^{}-1 into
  its base (35\% of the heat it absorbs; the enthalpy flux there is 120 times
  smaller). The Photochem column at the level is an isotherm at the clima
  skin temperature with no energy equation; an LTE thin estimate gives a
  warming of 120-240 K within 0.2-0.4 H below the level. The one-way
  coupling is not justified for this planet.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Corrected in the tree.} The ENERGY comment of
\texttt{recombination\_radiation\_absorbed} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}) listed He II
Ly-alpha on H I (27.2 eV) among the channels above the 30 eV threshold and
omitted the He III ground capture on He I, H2 and the metal ions; it now
lists them. The comment of \texttt{conduction\_base\_level\_T} called the isentropic
continuation the lower atmosphere\textquotesingle s own temperature; it now states that
this holds only for an adiabatic lower atmosphere and gives the measured
3.5 K offset from the profile isotherm. The catalog publisher
\texttt{.P1/publish\_rows/publish\_solved.sh} wrote its EW (percent-angstrom) under
the key \texttt{he10830\_red\_pair\_equivalent\_width\_mA}; the key is now
\texttt{...\_percent\_angstrom}. The 59 run.json records already published keep the
old key (held for the user).

\textbf{Held for the user.} The bath temperature itself (profile vs isentrope),
the 10.2-30 eV electron partition (Xu \& McCray 1991 not in \texttt{references/}),
the energy coupling to Photochem, and the ion-neutral drift: decisions, see
the report of this date.

\subsection{67. Phase 2: carried ionization stages certify on LHS 1140 b (2026-10-03)}\label{67-phase-2-carried-ionization-stages-certify-on-lhs-1140-b-2026-10-03}

\texttt{.P2/ew\_fit/phase2/NOTES\_phase2.md}. The failed 2026-09-23 attempt
(\texttt{.P1/iontrans\_atomic\_kzz1e9\_HeH2.13}: He++ carrier row 2.0e-2 at cells
416-419, omega halved to 0.125) was re-run from its own state, mapped onto
the face-at-level grid, on EXHALE\_f036290f.x, with the 09-23 conditions
(conduction off, \texttt{Interdiffusion\ enthalpy\ flux:\ False}). With
\texttt{EXHALE\_DIFF\_OMEGA=1.0} it certifies at outer pass 11
(\texttt{phase2/C\_p40}, \texttt{certified=T}; checked here): He++ row 8.6e-6, H+ 2.3e-8,
He+ 5.2e-7, He/H 6.8e-9. \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_SER} does not change the outcome;
omega = 0.5 refuses (the outermost element row halves each pass while the
hydro solve fails on the cell-500 momentum row).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  The 09-23 failure was the state and the solver of that binary, not the
  He++ equation: the row\textquotesingle s terms show the locally balanced x(He III),
  steep outward, advected with no source (measure about half of d ln x per
  cell over 7-29 R\_p). Beyond about 3 R\_p the He III recombination time
  exceeds the flow time, so the stage is frozen, 20-50 times below the
  local root.
\item
  Physical effect of carrying the stages (no conduction): x(H II) at
  11 R\_p 0.34 -\textgreater{} 0.042, n\_e at 29 R\_p about 10 times lower, outer T 460 -\textgreater{}
  1175 K, edge velocity 1.2 -\textgreater{} 1.9 km/s, log Mdot +0.012 dex; unchanged
  inside about 1.5 R\_p.
\item
  EW of the certified state (MEASURED here, \texttt{phase2/ew/C\_p40}): solution
  spectrum 1.0483 percent-angstrom (status result), advected product
  1.0182 (conditional). The two agree within 3\% once the stages are
  carried, against a factor 11 between the solution and advected EWs of a
  local-balance state (section 66): the bracketing-on-adv, accepting-on-
  solution route of the plan\textquotesingle s decision 2 is consistent.
\item
  Open (proposed, not coded): the element operator\textquotesingle s outer face carries
  zero diffusive flux (\texttt{binary\_element\_diffusion.f90} 219-228, 3245-3250),
  a wall condition at an open outflow; at the top D\_12 = 3.7e13 cm\^{}2/s and
  Pe = 2.8, so it makes a 1-2 cell layer (X\_He -0.30\% at cell 500). An
  outflow condition (gradient by the zero-gradient ghost, settling and
  thermal drift kept with the cell-N coefficients) is proposed as a
  default-off option, in phase 4. With the enthalpy flux on the hydro solve
  fails on the cell-500 momentum row from pass 7 (\texttt{phase2/G\_from\_C\_enthon},
  stopped): phase 4.
\item
  Comment corrected: \texttt{carrier\_row\_terms\_write} (\texttt{diffusive\_photochemistry.f90})
  described two printed scales; it prints one, \texttt{rowdump\_scale}, as its file
  header says, and the helium-stage production and loss columns are the
  positive and negative parts of the net source (checked at
  \texttt{carrier\_source}).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{69. The 10.2-30 eV photoelectron partition of Furlanetto \& Stoever (2010), a default-off option (2026-10-03)}\label{69-the-102-30-ev-photoelectron-partition-of-furlanetto--stoever-2010-a-default-off-option-2026-10-03}

Photoelectrons of 10.2-30 eV were deposited entirely as heat (\texttt{E\_sec\_ion},
the bottom of the Dalgarno, Yan \& Liu 1999 tables), although above 10.2 eV
they excite and ionize H I and He I. New key
\texttt{Low-energy\ electron\ partition:\ Furlanetto2010} (default \texttt{none}, the
previous behavior); notes \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/sec\_partition/NOTES.md}.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Source: Furlanetto \& Stoever 2010, MNRAS 404, 1869 (published PDF in
  \texttt{references/}), and the authors\textquotesingle{} electronic tables (paper footnote 9;
  the archive \texttt{elec\_interp.tar.gz} is no longer on the authors\textquotesingle{} site, the
  copy used is the Wayback Machine snapshot of 2017-11-02, SHA-1 equal to
  the archive\textquotesingle s CDX digest; \texttt{references/Furlanetto\_2010\_tables/}). Rows
  10.0-32.2 eV at all 14 ionized fractions are carried as parameter arrays in
  the generated \texttt{src/modules/radiation/low\_energy\_electron\_tables.f90}
  (generator \texttt{src/utils/furlanetto2010\_tables\_to\_fortran.py}, which checks
  the archive digest and the bytes of every table file). The physics is in
  \texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_degradation.f90}; one routine decides for every bin
  which of the three bands (above 30 eV, 10.2-30 eV, below 10.2 eV) a
  photoelectron falls in, for the stellar and the recombination paths
  alike.
\item
  Interpolation as the authors\textquotesingle{} \texttt{elec\_interp.c} (linear in E and in x),
  except two stated differences: everything below 10.2 eV is heat (the
  paper\textquotesingle s section 7), and x is clamped to the table range {[}1e-4, 0.999{]}.
  Against the C code at 3042 (E, x) points: largest differences 2.6e-7
  (f\_heat), 3.4e-7 (n\_Lya), 7.7e-8 (n\_ion,HI). Energy closure: the tables
  themselves close to 2.7e-5; the code\textquotesingle s partition to 3.6e-5 on rows and
  9.8e-5 between rows. Xu \& McCray 1991 (ApJ 375, 190, the ADS scan of the
  published article) Figs. 4-5 agree qualitatively.
\item
  Accounting: heat f\_heat E0; n\_ion,HI and n\_ion,HeI added to the existing
  secondary-ionization rates (n\_ion,HeII is zero below 30 eV); the
  excitation energy leaves as line radiation, as the existing partition
  treats its non-heat, non-ionization share (Ly-alpha trapping in thick
  H I is not followed: the heat is a lower bound there). Branching between
  H I and He I renormalized to the cell\textquotesingle s composition by the existing rule
  from the tables\textquotesingle{} He/H 0.087. Cells with n(H2) \textgreater{} 0 keep the heat-only
  treatment (no source covers H2 at 10-30 eV).
\item
  Restart contract: a token \texttt{lowE\_partition} on the \texttt{\#\ options} line, so a
  state is not silently restarted under the other setting; old states read
  as F. Every state file now carries \texttt{lowE\_partition=F} when off.
\item
  Default path: control and measured builds give identical numerical
  outputs on \texttt{wasp\_he23off} (20 steps) and \texttt{hp\_front} (100 steps), with and
  without \texttt{Secondary\_ionization:\ Immediate}; physics\_probe 2295 PASS; the new
  suite \texttt{src/tests/low\_energy\_electron\_degradation} 14 PASS (rerun here).
\item
  Discontinuity at 30 eV where the source changes (atomic gas): Delta f\_heat
  -0.011 (x = 1e-4), -0.006 (1e-2), -0.002 (0.1), 0 (0.5, 0.9); not
  smoothed (no source).
\item
  Option on: \texttt{wasp\_he23off} heating -0.67\%, P\_HI +0.63\%, T -0.41\% at the
  ionization front (1.03-1.05 R\_p). On the molecular LHS 1140 b and
  hot-Uranus states every cell has n(H2) \textgreater{} 0, so the main solve is unchanged,
  while the advected post-process, which rebuilds an H/He gas without
  molecules, applies it everywhere (He/H 9 conduction state: adv T -2.9\%,
  heat -12\%, He 2\^{}3S +3.8\%). This inconsistency of the gate is held for the
  user. \texttt{md/input\_schema.md} K14c\textquotesingle s "E0 \textgreater{} 40 eV" corrected to 30 eV.
\item
  Not changed, reported: photoelectrons above 30 eV on the recombination path
  (He III ground-capture channels) are still deposited as heat, where the
  stellar path partitions them.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{70. The conduction bath below the level is the profile\textquotesingle s temperature (2026-10-03)}\label{70-the-conduction-bath-below-the-level-is-the-profiles-temperature-2026-10-03}

User decision. \texttt{base\_reservoir\_temperature\_at} (\texttt{base\_boundary.f90}), the
bath temperature the conduction operator holds at the base ghost
(\texttt{conduction\_base\_level\_T}), continued the reservoir\textquotesingle s hydrostatic isentrope
below the level even where a lower-atmosphere profile is handed over; on
the LHS 1140 b photochem profiles the column there is an isotherm at the
clima skin temperature, and the isentrope put the bath 3.5 K above it
(188.89 against 185.42 K), lowering the conductive flux into the level by
about 4.6\% at a fixed cell-1 temperature (section 66). With a profile it now
returns the profile\textquotesingle s temperature at that radius, through a new accessor
\texttt{lap\_value\_at\_radius} (\texttt{lower\_atmosphere\_profile.f90}, linear in r between
the bracketing levels, nearer end outside); without a profile, the
isentrope as before. Comment of \texttt{conduction\_base\_level\_T} updated.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Checked on the certified He/H 2.09 state with conduction and carried
  stages (\texttt{phase3/iontrans\_diag/B2\_hold0.32}), evaluate-only, scratch ifx
  build of the tree (md5 3c236561) against EXHALE\_f036290f.x
  (\texttt{.P2/ew\_fit/bath\_T/}): the only row that moves is the energy row of cell
  1 (1.6e-8 -\textgreater{} 4.4e-2 of its scale, refused); every other row equal to the
  digits printed. The state is to be re-solved with the fixes now in
  progress, then the matching-level temperature sensitivity (plan section 8).
\item
  Runs without a profile, and the base ghost state of the advection (its own
  isentrope in \texttt{base\_ghost\_averages}), are unchanged.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{71. One H2 gate for both products; recombination photoelectrons partitioned as on the stellar path (2026-10-03)}\label{71-one-h2-gate-for-both-products-recombination-photoelectrons-partitioned-as-on-the-stellar-path-2026-10-03}

Notes: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/sec\_partition2/NOTES.md}.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{H2 gate (user decision).} The 10.2-30 eV partition of section 69 is
  gated by n(H2) \textgreater{} 0 in one place (\texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_partition}); every
  path now passes the SOLVED state\textquotesingle s H2 density (the advected post-process
  passes \texttt{nmol\_eq(:,1)}, the same column its \texttt{\_adv} files carry). On the
  He/H 9 conduction state (500 of 500 cells with H2) option on and off now
  give identical solved and advected products in every cell (before: the
  advected product differed in 329 cells); on the atomic \texttt{wasp\_he23off} the
  option acts as before (advected heat -0.67\%, x(H+) -4.6\%).
\item
  \textbf{Recombination photoelectrons (defect).} They were deposited whole as
  heat; above \texttt{E\_sec\_ion} the stellar path partitions the same energies.
  Each recombination photoelectron is now passed, at its fixed
  representative energy, through the stellar path\textquotesingle s bands and partition
  (\texttt{photoelectron\_partition\_at\_energy}, the same bracket and interpolation
  as \texttt{photoelectron\_partition\_on\_grid}, 0-1.2e-16 apart), under the
  stellar path\textquotesingle s switch (\texttt{use\_sec\_ion\ .and.\ sec\_ion\_active}, the STAGED
  default included): f\_heat of it is heat, its H I, He I and H2
  ionizations go to dP\_HI, dP\_HeI, dP\_H2 (dissociative one per 22 H2+),
  the rest leaves as line radiation. Channels concerned: He III ground
  capture on H I, He I, H2 and metal ions with thresholds below 24.4 eV,
  and He II Ly-alpha on metal ions below 10.8 eV (Na I, Ca I, Mg I, Fe I;
  previously missing from the comment). Inner-shell metal absorptions
  partition the photoelectron and the Auger electron separately, as the
  stellar path does. With the 10.2-30 eV option on, the recombination path
  now uses the He I ground singlet as the He I target, as the stellar path
  does (it used the summed He I).
\item
  Tests: physics\_probe 2499 PASS, 0 FAIL (new driver
  \texttt{recombination\_photoelectron\_partition.f90}, 204 PASS: energy closure
  1.2e-15, partition-whole equals heat-only to 4.4e-16, gate closed bitwise
  with solved H2); \texttt{low\_energy\_electron\_degradation} 14 PASS; rerun here on
  the merged tree.
\item
  Effects (control vs measured, OMP 1): \texttt{wasp\_full} and \texttt{hp\_front} with the
  default staged coupling: numerically identical (the coupling is not yet
  switched on in these snapshots); with \texttt{Secondary\_ionization:\ Immediate}:
  wasp\_full heat -5.3e-5, T -2.7e-5, x(H+) +3.4e-5 at 1.036-1.041 R\_p
  (the He recombination heating channel itself -0.23\%); hp\_front below 1e-7.
  The certified He/H 2.09 transport state: wind energy row 8.4e-16 -\textgreater{}
  1.3e-11, gated H+ row 1.6e-8 -\textgreater{} 3.3e-8, far below tolerance.
\item
  Not traced: on the He/H 9 conduction state (option off) one cell, 118
  (1.035 R\_p, where n\_e falls off), moves He 2\^{}3S -1.2\% and He III +1.4\%;
  every other cell below 1.1e-4.
\item
  Limit: each channel is partitioned at its representative energy, not
  averaged over the capture continuum.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{72. The enthalpy of the carried ionization stages; the movement-bound rule (2026-10-03)}\label{72-the-enthalpy-of-the-carried-ionization-stages-the-movement-bound-rule-2026-10-03}

Notes: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/phase3/stage\_enthalpy/NOTES.md}; work in
a worktree, merged into the tree by a three-way merge (the two option tokens
added at the same time, \texttt{lowE\_partition} and \texttt{stage\_enth}, are both in the
vocabulary, n\_opt = 25, every token array checked at 25 entries).

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Stage enthalpy (\texttt{TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (j), now fixed for the stages).} The
  stage flux F\_k = x\_k N\_el + E\_k, E\_k = -n\_el K dx\_k/dr, moves the stage
  relative to its element by E\_k; by Cook (2009, eqs. 11-13) it carries
  q\_st = sum\_el sum\_k E\_k {[}h\_k - hbar\_el{]}, hbar\_el the enthalpy per nucleus
  of the species \texttt{carrier\_write\_back} rescales (hydrogen: H I, H2+, H3+;
  helium: He I with its 2\^{}3S; HeH+ never). With every heavy particle at
  the same sensible enthalpy the exchange moves only the electrons\textquotesingle:
  q\_st = h\_e sum\_k Z\_k E\_k, h\_e = gamma/(gamma-1) kT, exact for helium and
  corrected for the H2+/H3+ share of hydrogen. Routine
  \texttt{ionization\_stage\_enthalpy\_face\_flux}; the recoil set is formed once
  (\texttt{recoiling\_nucleus\_enthalpies}) for this and the molecular term
  (\texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_face\_flux} renamed \texttt{molecular\_carrier\_enthalpy\_face\_flux});
  \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_divergence\_of\_state} returns the sum, so the stationary
  residual, the marching update and the advected column energy solve take
  it from one routine. Under \texttt{Interdiffusion\ enthalpy\ flux} (default on),
  only with \texttt{Ionization\ transport:\ True}; state token \texttt{stage\_enth}. Validity
  at the code site: zero current, one temperature, no stage drift beyond
  E\_k (the ion-neutral drift and its enthalpy are not modeled), no Dufour
  term.
\item
  Tests: new \texttt{src/tests/ionization\_stage\_enthalpy\_flux/} 13 PASS (closed
  ends exactly 0, column sum 1.1e-16, manufactured q 7.7e-16, H2+/H3+
  recoil 2.3e-15, zero for uniform fractions, iontrans off and key off);
  \texttt{carrier\_enthalpy\_flux} 13 PASS, \texttt{ionization\_stage\_flux} 45 PASS; an
  independent recomputation from the B2 state files agrees with the
  production Sidf difference to 1.8e-13 of its maximum. Default path
  (\texttt{wasp\_he23off}) identical; \texttt{hp\_front} (iontrans on) moves by up to
  7.9e-6, identical with the key off, i.e. the new term.
\item
  On the certified He/H 2.09 transport state (B2): the term is up to
  1.7e-3 of the energy-row scale (1.05-1.2 R\_p), above 1e-6 in 369 of 500
  cells, about 12 times smaller than the phase-1 estimate on a local-balance
  state. Re-solved with it (\texttt{R\_B2\_stage\_hold0.32}): certified in 11 passes;
  T -6.2e-5, v -2.3e-4, x(H+) +1.5e-4, He 2\^{}3S +4.1e-4 at most; log Mdot
  7.80532 -\textgreater{} 7.80523; EW solution 0.5829 (was 0.5830), advected 0.5679.
\item
  \textbf{Movement-bound rule} (\texttt{bound\_step\_harmed}, \texttt{EXHALE\_main.f90}): a rise
  of the joint distance counts as harm of the step only if the update moved
  the cell that carries the joint distance by at least half of the largest
  movement (\texttt{prog\_cell}, \texttt{update\_count\_move}); otherwise the rise is the
  wind\textquotesingle s response there, logged as such, and the bound grows. In the refused
  run of section 68 the update had moved those cells by 0.002-0.17 of the
  largest movement. Without information the old rule applies. Verified:
  the He/H 2.09 iontrans switch without a trust hold now certifies in 28
  passes (refused at 12 before; the same solution as B2 to \textbar d ln n\textbar{} 4.7e-7);
  the atomic phase-2 route 11 passes and the conduction rung s = 0.32 -\textgreater{}
  0.64 28 passes, both bitwise equal to the control build.
\item
  Every iontrans state certified before this section (B2, B3, C, C2) solved
  the previous energy equation. The docs (\texttt{md/input\_schema.md} K44, the
  manual\textquotesingle s \texttt{Interdiffusion\ enthalpy\ flux} entry, \texttt{docs/physics\_overview/})
  are updated; \texttt{TO\_BE\_DONE.md} (j) is marked fixed for the stages.
\item
  \texttt{make\ test} on the merged tree: 45 of 48 entries PASS. Besides the two
  steady suites that need \texttt{EXHALE\_STEADY\_RUNLOGS}, \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} fails
  \texttt{stationary\_evaluate\_*}: the stored certified state of
  \texttt{backup/regression/wasp\_full\_newton}, evaluated with the secondary
  ionization armed, now moves OIII by 6.0e-5 and is refused. With the
  control build of section 71 (before the recombination photoelectron
  partition) the same test passes (3.8e-10, certified, exit 0): the stored
  state is a solution of the previous equations. Its refresh belongs with
  the deferred refreshes.
\item
  Merged ifx binary for the next runs: \texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/EXHALE\_6af10ba4.x}
  (manifest \texttt{.P2/BINARY\_MANIFEST\_6af10ba4*.txt}). The He/H 2.09 reference
  is being re-solved on it from B2 (\texttt{...\_HeH2.09\_ref\_merged01}, no trust
  hold, \texttt{Restart\ option\ change:\ stage\_enth}).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{74. Photochem/wind elemental-flux closure on the new physics (2026-10-03)}\label{74-photochemwind-elemental-flux-closure-on-the-new-physics-2026-10-03}

\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/phase3/closure/NOTES.md}; driver unchanged
(\texttt{element\_flux\_closure.py} passes the reference\textquotesingle s keys and env; checked in
the running process environment), binary 6af10ba4, Photochem 0.9.0 with
climate on, omega 0.5, tolerance 5\%.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Iteration 0 (the reference\textquotesingle s profile, trial H 2.566e7, He 5.335e7 g/s from
  the no-conduction closure): measured H 2.1209e7, He 4.2318e7 g/s (overlap
  window, spread 2e-7): eps 0.21 / 0.26. With conduction and carried stages
  the wind takes 17\% less H and 21\% less He.
\item
  Converged at k = 2: eps 0.026 / 0.033 below 5\%, window spread \textless{} 1e-7;
  three wind solves certified (4-6 passes each), log Mdot 7.800.
\item
  The measured fluxes do not respond to the trial: over the iterations they
  moved by 1e-5 while the trial moved by 15-18\%; eps fell only by the
  damping. The fixed point is the measured flux (H 2.12095e7, He 4.23172e7
  g/s); the accepted trial is 2.6\% / 3.3\% above it (one undamped step
  closes it, for phase 6).
\item
  At the 1 microbar match: He/H -1.7e-5, T unchanged, q\_H2 +1.4e-5; the
  column above changes by at most 0.16\%; the wind by \textless{} 1e-5 (He 2\^{}3S
  -4.5e-4 near the H2 front). EW of k02: solution 0.5793, advected 0.5643,
  equal to the reference to the four digits recorded.
\item
  Not done: the undamped step; the Kn, Mach and HeH+ Da profiles of the
  final state; the reproduction of the 2026-09-16 profile by the present
  adapter.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{75. The EW depends on the matching-level temperature: the energy coupling to Photochem is required (2026-10-03)}\label{75-the-ew-depends-on-the-matching-level-temperature-the-energy-coupling-to-photochem-is-required-2026-10-03}

\texttt{LHS1140b/models/.P2/ew\_fit/phase3/T0\_sensitivity/NOTES.md}. On the He/H
2.09 reference (section 73) the lower-atmosphere profile was raised by
Delta T at and above the match and tapered below it with the column
response phase 1 solved (all-band shape, 1/e 0.19 H, for 120 K;
rotational-band shape, 1/e 0.41 H, for 240 K), levels inserted every 0.02 H
to 3 H, r re-integrated hydrostatically (the match rises by 2.6e-4 and
1.1e-3 R\_p, so R0 changes), composition and K\_zz held at the reference
values; headers marked as diagnostic modifications. The reference state
was mapped onto each new grid (\texttt{map\_state\_to\_grid.py\ -\/-ic}) and re-solved
with the reference controls; both certified (24 and 21 passes).
